                    + '&url=' + encodeURIComponent(location.href.replace(/^http(s)?:\/\/(www\.)?/, ''));
                var referrer = document.referrer;
                if (referrer) {
                    beatUrl += '&ref=' + encodeURIComponent(referrer);
                }
                var match = document.cookie.match(/_wixCIDX=([^;]*)/)
                if (match) {
                    beatUrl += '&client_id=' + match[1];
                }
                var prevMark = 'fetchStart';
                return function(et, name, extra, pageNumber) {
                    var tts = Math.round(performance.now());
                    var ts = et === 1 ? 0 : Date.now() - wixBiSession.initialTimestamp;
                    if (name && performance.mark) {
                        var mark = name + ' (beat ' + et + ')';
                        performance.mark(mark);
                        if (performance.measure) {
                            performance.measure('\u2B50' + mark, prevMark, mark);
                        }
                        prevMark = mark;
                    }
                    extra = extra || '';
                    if (extra.indexOf('pn=') === -1) {
                        extra += '&pn=' + (pageNumber || '1');
                    }
                    if (extra.indexOf('sr=') === -1 && screen.width) {
                        extra += '&sr=' + screen.width + 'x' + screen.height;
                    }
                    if (screen.availWidth) {
                        extra += '&sar=' + screen.availWidth + 'x' + screen.availHeight;
                    }
                    if (extra.indexOf('wr=') === -1 && window.innerWidth) {
                        extra += '&wr=' + window.innerWidth + 'x' + window.innerHeight;
                    }
                    if (window.outerWidth) {
                        extra += '&wor=' + window.outerWidth + 'x' + window.outerHeight;
                    }
                    if (extra.indexOf('ita=') === -1) {
                        extra += '&ita=' + (wixBiSession.checkVisibility() ? '1' : '0');
                    }
                    if (wixBiSession.siteRevision || wixBiSession.siteCacheRevision) {
                        extra += '&siterev=' + wixBiSession.siteRevision + "-" + wixBiSession.siteCacheRevision;
                    }
                    wixBiSession.sendBeacon(beatUrl
                        + '&et=' + et
                        + (name ? '&event_name=' + encodeURIComponent(name) : '')
                        + '&ts=' + ts
                        + '&tts=' + tts
                        + '&vsi=' + wixBiSession.viewerSessionId
                        + '&rid=' + wixBiSession.requestId
                        + '&viewer_name=' + encodeURIComponent(wixBiSession.renderType)
                        + '&is_rollout=' + wixBiSession.is_rollout
                        + '&is_platform_loaded=' + wixBiSession.is_platform_loaded
                        + (wixBiSession.sessionId ? '&sessionId=' + wixBiSession.sessionId  : '')
                        + (wixBiSession.visitorId ? '&vid=' + wixBiSession.visitorId  : '')
                        + (wixBiSession.siteMemberId ? '&mid=' + wixBiSession.siteMemberId  : '')
                        + '&is_cached=' + wixBiSession.isCached
                        + '&caching=' + wixBiSession.caching
                        + extra
                    );
                };
            })()
        };

        (function () {
            var microPop, caching = 'none';
            var match = document.cookie.match(/ssr-caching="cache,\s*desc=(\w+)(?:,\s*varnish=(\w+))?(?:,\s*dc,\s*desc=(\w+))?(?:"|;|$)/);
            if (!match && window.PerformanceServerTiming) {
                match = [];
                var serverTiming = performance.getEntriesByType('navigation')[0].serverTiming;
                serverTiming.forEach(function (st) {
                    switch (st.name) {
                        case 'cache':
                            match[1] = st.description;
                            break;
                        case 'varnish':
                            match[2] = st.description;
                            break;
                        case 'dc':
                            microPop = st.description;
                    }
                });
            }
            if (match && match.length) {
                caching = match[1] + ',' + (match[2] || 'none');
                if (!microPop) {
                    microPop = match[3];
                }
            }
            wixBiSession.caching = caching;
            wixBiSession.isCached = caching.indexOf("hit") === 0;
            if (microPop) {
                wixBiSession.microPop = microPop;
            }
        })();

        wixBiSession.sendBeat(1, 'Init');
    </script>

style="top:;bottom:;left:;right:;width:969px;height:auto;position:;min-height:235px;pointer-events:none"><p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:メイリオ,meiryo,ヒラギノ角ゴ pro w3,hiragino kaku gothic pro,sans-serif;"><span>​</span></span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span style="font-size:21px;">日本の伝統、和の美しさを「伝統を崩すことなく」「新し­い切り口で」</span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; line-height:1.6em; text-align:center;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span style="font-size:21px;">魅せることを目標に2012年フランスはストラスブール公演をキッカケに立ち上げられました。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; line-height:1.6em; text-align:center;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span style="font-size:21px;">日舞の艶やかさに加え現代舞踊、現代音楽をミックスしたスピーディな展開、</span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; line-height:1.6em; text-align:center;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span style="font-size:21px;">気鋭の振り­付け師による演出とアート性。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; line-height:1.6em; text-align:center;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span style="font-size:21px;">国内外で「カッコいいニッポン」を意識するキッカケになればと活動しています。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; line-height:1.6em; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:メイリオ,meiryo,ヒラギノ角ゴ pro w3,hiragino kaku gothic pro,sans-serif;"><span class="wixGuard">​</span></span></span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;">Notre but est de présenter la culture traditionnelle japonaise à réaliser la contribution au monde.</span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;">Nous vous proposons de nouvelles pensées et valeurs</span></p>
<p class="font_8" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;">C'est la philosophie  :  "Ôénza".</span></p></div><div class="v1" id="comp-jt4ocn86" style="height:580px;width:957px;top:;bottom:;left:;right:;position:"><div class="v1videoFrame" id="comp-jt4ocn86videoFrame" tabindex="0"><iframe allowfullscreen="" aria-label="External YouTube" data-src="//www.youtube.com/embed/U0gSeLgJlFE?wmode=transparent&amp;autoplay=0&amp;theme=dark&amp;controls=1&amp;autohide=0&amp;loop=0&amp;showinfo=0&amp;rel=0&amp;playlist=false&amp;enablejsapi=0" frameborder="0" height="100%" title="External YouTube" width="100%"></iframe></div><div class="v1preview" id="comp-jt4ocn86preview" style="display:none"></div></div><div class="v1" id="comp-jqs5efui" style="height:580px;width:957px;top:;bottom:;left:;right:;position:"><div class="v1videoFrame" id="comp-jqs5efuivideoFrame" tabindex="0"><iframe allowfullscreen="" aria-label="External YouTube" data-src="//www.youtube.com/embed/iA4wLMfGXy8?wmode=transparent&amp;autoplay=0&amp;theme=dark&amp;controls=1&amp;autohide=0&amp;loop=0&amp;showinfo=0&amp;rel=0&amp;playlist=false&amp;enablejsapi=0" frameborder="0" height="100%" title="External YouTube" width="100%"></iframe></div><div class="v1preview" id="comp-jqs5efuipreview" style="display:none"></div></div><div class="txtNew" data-min-height="823" data-packed="false" id="comp-jqs5l9hx" style="top:;bottom:;left:;right:;width:969px;height:auto;position:;min-height:823px;pointer-events:none"><p class="font_7" style="text-align:center;"><span style="color:#000000;"><span style="font-style:normal;"><span style="font-weight:400;"><span style="letter-spacing:0.15em;"><span style="font-family:poppins-semibold,poppins,sans-serif;"><span style="font-size:78px;">en Design</span></span></span></span></span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"> </p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">気品と艶っぽさを醸し出すお引きずりで、</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">提灯を怪しく灯す。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span class="wixGuard">​</span></span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">日舞とジャズコンテンポラリーを融合させた振付で、独特な世界観を演出するen Design。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><br/>
<span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">日本人の表には出さない内面の強さや、相手との距離感、曖昧さ。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">見えないものを見ようとする想像力。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span class="wixGuard">​</span></span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">聴きづらいものを聴こうとする思いやり…</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><br/>
<span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">決して派手ではない部分を日本人独特の「間」を用いて表現。<br/>
古くからの伝統を守りつつ、新しい伝統を創り出す世界。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><br/>
<span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">お引きずりでパフォーマンスをする世界で唯一のチーム。</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">「en Design 」。<br/>
 </span></span><br/>
 </p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-weight:bold;"><span style="font-size:67px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">花魁道中</span></span></span><br/>
 </p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">春の夜や大名衆も知らぬ夢</span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;"><span class="wixGuard">​</span></span></span></p>
<p class="font_7" style="font-size:21px; text-align:center;"><span style="font-size:21px;"><span style="font-family:ｍｓ ｐ明朝,ms pmincho,ヒラギノ明朝 pro w3,hiragino mincho pro,serif;">本格的な花魁が禿（かむろ）と新造を従え、江戸の華やかな花魁道中を再現。<br/>
25㎝の高下駄で八文字歩きをする姿は圧巻。<br/>
また華やかで、力強い舞も披露します。</span></span></p></div><div class="style-jqs7kx7k" data-height-diff="0" data-is-mesh="true" data-margin="1" data-max-rows="10" data-num-cols="4" data-state="hidePlayButton
